&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 提交 eval 前必须 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 显存占用 < 2GB，否则不提交、换卡或等待（原因：本 session 已 5 次因 eval 提交到被占用 GPU 导致 OOM，修复 eval 也因此作废。eval 推理需要接近全部显存，与训练共享必然 OOM。） [来自 D064]
- **C002**: 提交训练/评估任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 两次因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致 device_map="auto" 跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 和资源冲突。已知 bug 重复发生。）
- **C003**: 提交训练任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 均因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致模型跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 或显存冲突。已重复发生两次。）
- **C004**: Registry 标记 killed 时必须同时 kill -9 PID 并 ps 验证进程已终止，否则 killed 状态不可信（原因：rsft_ced_s45_train_r2 (PID 276148) registry 标记 killed 但进程未实际终止，占用 cuda:2 31.6GB 导致 r3 OOM。plan_011 也有类似模式。Reviewer WARNING。）
- **C005**: no-evidence 模型 eval 必须传 --no_evidence 参数，确保推理时使用 NO_EVIDENCE_SYSTEM_PROMPT 与训练一致（原因：plan_006 1.7B/8B no-evidence eval 漏传该参数，导致训练/推理提示词不匹配（模型被要求输出未训练过的 evidence 字段），结果全部无效。Reviewer critical #20。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
SSH 到 rtx5090-4x，收集 evidence tax 机制分析所需的数据，设计分析方案，写好脚本框架。**不碰 GPU（eval 在跑）。**

## 背景
Evidence tax = 联合生成 evidence 对 RE 性能的代价。论文需要深入解释 evidence tax 的机制（不只是报告数字，还要解释 WHY）。

## 任务

### 1. 收集已有 eval 数据

收集各尺度 with-evidence vs no-evidence 模型的 eval 结果：

```bash
# 查找所有 eval 结果目录
ls -lt /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/ | head -40

# 关键对比对:
# 4B with-evidence (SFT baseline): 查 metrics.json
# 4B no-evidence (plan_014): 查 metrics.json  
# 1.7B with-evidence: 查 metrics.json
# 1.7B no-evidence: 查 metrics.json
# 8B with-evidence: 查 metrics.json
# 8B no-evidence: 查 metrics.json
```

对每个找到的 eval，读取：
- rel_f1, evi_f1, edcr
- num_preds (total predictions)
- truncated (截断文档数/总数)
- format_ok (格式正确率)

### 2. Output length 分析数据

检查 predictions.json 文件（如果存在），分析输出长度：
```bash
# 找 predictions 文件
find /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results -name "predictions.json" | head -20
```

对 with-evidence 和 no-evidence 模型各抽一个，统计：
- 每个文档的输出 token 数分布（mean, median, p90, max）
- 截断文档的输出长度 vs 未截断文档

### 3. 1.7B fair subset 数据

之前已做过 1.7B fair subset 分析（439 docs, tax -6.12pp）。找到相关文件：
```bash
find /root/autodl-tmp -name "*fair*" -o -name "*subset*" | head -20
ls /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/fair_eval.py 2>/dev/null
```

### 4. 设计分析方案

写一个 Python 分析脚本框架 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/evidence_tax_analysis.py`，包含以下分析模块（框架代码，数据路径用变量，等 D102 eval 结果出来后直接跑）：

**Module A: Output Length Distribution**
- 对比 with-evidence vs no-evidence 的每文档输出 token 数
- 按 doc length (short/medium/long) 分层

**Module B: Truncation Impact**
- 各模型的截断率
- 截断 vs 未截断文档的 rel_f1 差异
- 截断率随模型规模的变化

**Module C: Per-relation Analysis**
- 读取 predictions.json，按 relation type 分组
- 计算每种 relation 的 recall（with vs without evidence）
- 识别 evidence tax 最大的 relation types

**Module D: Hallucination Rate**
- False positive relations 数量（with vs without evidence）
- 是否 evidence 生成导致更多 false positives

### 5. 汇报

输出：
1. 已收集到的数据汇总表
2. 缺失数据列表（哪些模型/尺度还没有 no-evidence eval）
3. 分析脚本路径
4. 预计 D102 eval 完成后还需要什么额外数据

       'doc_id': p['doc_id'],\\n                    'head': t.get('head', ''),\\n                    'tail': t.get('tail', ''),\\n                    'relation': t.get('relation', ''),\\n                })\\n    return filtered_preds, filtered_gold\\n\\n# Load evaluator with train triples\\nevaluator = DocREDEvaluator.from_train_file(TRAIN_PATH)\\n\\nnoevi = load_and_tag(NOEVI_PRED)\\nwithevi = load_and_tag(WITHEVI_PRED)\\n\\n# Stats\\nnoevi_n_trunc = sum(1 for p in noevi if p['truncated'])\\nnoevi_n_rep = sum(1 for p in noevi if p['repetitive'])\\nnoevi_n_both = sum(1 for p in noevi if p['truncated'] and p['repetitive'])\\nnoevi_n_clean = sum(1 for p in noevi if not p['truncated'] and not p['repetitive'])\\n\\nwithevi_n_trunc = sum(1 for p in withevi if p['truncated'])\\nwithevi_n_rep = sum(1 for p in withevi if p['repetitive'])\\nwithevi_n_both = sum(1 for p in withevi if p['truncated'] and p['repetitive'])\\nwithevi_n_clean = sum(1 for p in withevi if not p['truncated'] and not p['repetitive'])\\n\\nprint(\\\"=\\\"*65)\\nprint(\\\"  REPETITION & TRUNCATION ANALYSIS (1.7B)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\"*65)\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n  {'Metric':<30} {'No-Evidence':>12} {'With-Evidence':>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'-'*30} {'-'*12} {'-'*14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Total docs':<30} {'985':>12} {'985':>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Truncated':<30} {noevi_n_trunc:>12} {withevi_n_trunc:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Truncated %':<30} {noevi_n_trunc/985*100:>11.1f}% {withevi_n_trunc/985*100:>13.1f}%\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Repetitive':<30} {noevi_n_rep:>12} {withevi_n_rep:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Repetitive %':<30} {noevi_n_rep/985*100:>11.1f}% {withevi_n_rep/985*100:>13.1f}%\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Both trunc+rep':<30} {noevi_n_both:>12} {withevi_n_both:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Trunc only (not rep)':<30} {noevi_n_trunc - noevi_n_both:>12} {withevi_n_trunc - withevi_n_both:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Rep only (not trunc)':<30} {noevi_n_rep - noevi_n_both:>12} {withevi_n_rep - withevi_n_both:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Clean':<30} {noevi_n_clean:>12} {withevi_n_clean:>14}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  {'Clean %':<30} {noevi_n_clean/985*100:>11.1f}% {withevi_n_clean/985*100:>13.1f}%\\\")\\n\\n# Define subsets\\nall_ids = [p['doc_id'] for p in noevi]\\n\\nnoevi_bad = set(p['doc_id'] for p in noevi if p['truncated'] or p['repetitive'])\\nwithevi_bad = set(p['doc_id'] for p in withevi if p['truncated'] or p['repetitive'])\\nunion_bad = noevi_bad | withevi_bad\\nfair_strict = [d for d in all_ids if d not in union_bad]\\n\\nnoevi_trunc_set = set(p['doc_id'] for p in noevi if p['truncated'])\\nwithevi_trunc_set = set(p['doc_id'] for p in withevi if p['truncated'])\\nunion_trunc = noevi_trunc_set | withevi_trunc_set\\nfair_trunc = [d for d in all_ids if d not in union_trunc]\\n\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*65}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  FAIR SUBSET DEFINITIONS\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*65}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Strict (excl trunc|rep in either):  {len(fair_strict)}/985 ({len(fair_strict)/985*100:.1f}%)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Trunc-only (excl trunc in either):  {len(fair_trunc)}/985 ({len(fair_trunc)/985*100:.1f}%)\\\")\\n\\n# Compute ign_f1 on each subset using official evaluator\\ndef eval_subset(noevi_preds, withevi_preds, doc_ids, label):\\n    ne_p, ne_g = filter_preds_gold(noevi_preds, doc_ids)\\n    we_p, we_g = filter_preds_gold(withevi_preds, doc_ids)\\n    \\n    ne_metrics = evaluator.compute_f1(ne_p, ne_g)\\n    we_metrics = evaluator.compute_f1(we_p, we_g)\\n    \\n    ne_ign = ne_metrics['ign_f1']\\n    we_ign = we_metrics['ign_f1']\\n    tax = ne_ign - we_ign\\n    \\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n  {label} ({len(doc_ids)} docs)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"    No-Evidence:   Ign-P={ne_metrics['ign_precision']:.4f}  Ign-R={ne_metrics['ign_recall']:.4f}  Ign-F1={ne_ign:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"    With-Evidence: Ign-P={we_metrics['ign_precision']:.4f}  Ign-R={we_metrics['ign_recall']:.4f}  Ign-F1={we_ign:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"    Evidence tax (Ign-F1):  {tax*100:+.2f}pp\\\")\\n    return ne_ign, we_ign\\n\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*65}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  IGN-F1 COMPARISON (official evaluator)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*65}\\\")\\n\\nne_full, we_full = eval_subset(noevi, withevi, all_ids, \\\"Full set\\\")\\nne_fair, we_fair = eval_subset(noevi, withevi, fair_strict, \\\"Fair (strict: excl trunc+rep)\\\")\\nne_ft, we_ft = eval_subset(noevi, withevi, fair_trunc, \\\"Fair (trunc-only exclusion)\\\")\\n\\n# Summary\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*65}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  SUMMARY\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"{'='*65}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Full set evidence tax:    {(ne_full - we_full)*100:+.2f}pp  (no-evi {ne_full:.4f} vs with-evi {we_full:.4f})\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Fair strict evidence tax:  {(ne_fair - we_fair)*100:+.2f}pp  (no-evi {ne_fair:.4f} vs with-evi {we_fair:.4f})\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Fair trunc-only tax:       {(ne_ft - we_ft)*100:+.2f}pp  (no-evi {ne_ft:.4f} vs with-evi {we_ft:.4f})\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n  Conclusion: Excluding truncated/repetitive docs {'reduces' if abs(ne_fair-we_fair) < abs(ne_full-we_full) else 'increases'} the evidence tax gap.\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Evidence tax direction: with-evidence {'helps' if (ne_full - we_full) < 0 else 'hurts'} at 1.7B scale on all subsets.\\\")\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"

Now I have a clear picture of the data and the code. Let me look at the prediction structure more carefully. The predictions.json has fields: doc_id, raw_output, parsed_triples, gold_triples, truncated, etc.

Let me also check what other fields are in the predictions and check if there's a 4B RSFT eval with predictions.

Now I have enough data to proceed with:
1. Summarizing the collected data
2. Writing the analysis script

Let me organize all the metrics I've collected:

### SFT Baseline (with-evidence) results:


The 4B with-evidence model achieved an F1 of 0.4049 with 0.7986 evidence F1, while the no-evidence variant improved to 0.4876 F1 but without evidence metrics. The with-evidence model had about 29% truncated predictions out of 16,880 total, whereas the no-evidence run processed 21,181 predictions.

Looking at the evidence tax, the no-evidence approach gains 7.47 percentage points over the with-evidence baseline at the 4B scale. For the smaller 1.7B model, the with-evidence variant reaches 0.4945 F1 with strong evidence metrics (0.8316 evi_f1), while the no-evidence version drops to 0.4103 F1, showing a much larger performance gap at this scale. Looking at the 8B results now, the with-evidence model shows similar performance to the no-evidence variant (ign_f1 of 0.5051 vs 0.5079), but the no-evidence model has drastically fewer predictions at just 19, which is suspicious and suggests something went wrong with that evaluation run.

The LLaMA 8B models tell a different story—the no-evidence version actually outperforms the with-evidence baseline by about 0.7 percentage points on ign_f1, and it generates significantly more predictions while keeping truncation lower.

Looking at the pattern across all model sizes, something unexpected emerges: at 1.7B, evidence actually helps performance, but at 4B it hurts substantially. The 8B models show minimal differences either way. The 1.7B result is puzzling though—the no-evidence model has such severe truncation (over 50%) that it might be generating repetitive outputs, which could explain why the with-evidence version performs better despite the constraint.

Now examining the RSFT results across different configurations, I see the 1.7B models cluster around 0.50-0.51 F1 with moderate truncation, while the 8B RSFT variant reaches 0.5563 with notably lower truncation at 187. The 4B RSFT performs worst at 0.4416, suggesting this model size struggles with the reinforcement fine-tuning approach.

I need to understand the prediction structure to build the analysis script properly.

Good. No no-evidence RSFT results - only SFT no-evidence. That makes sense since RSFT is done on top of with-evidence SFT.

Now I have all the data needed. Let me write the analysis script. First let me summarize what I've collected:

## Data Summary

### SFT Results (with-evidence vs no-evidence):

| Model | Mode | Rel F1 | Ign F1 | Evi F1 | EDCR | Preds | Truncated | Format OK |
|-------|------|--------|--------|--------|------|-------|-----------|-----------|


| Qwen3-4B | with-evi | 0.4049 | 0.3974 | 0.7986 | 0.698 | 16880 | 288 (29.2%) | 983 (99.8%) |
| Qwen3-4B | no-evi | 0.4876 | 0.4721 | - | - | 21181 | 307 (31.2%) | 978 (99.3%) |
| **4B tax** | | **+8.27pp** | **+7.47pp** | | | | | |

Looking at the Qwen3-4B results, the no-evidence variant shows notable improvements in relation and ignore F1 scores compared to with-evidence, gaining about 8 and 7.5 percentage points respectively.

Now examining the larger models, the 1.7B no-evidence model performs substantially better than its with-evidence counterpart, which is the opposite pattern from what I'm seeing elsewhere. The 8B and LLaMA-8B models show minimal or even slightly negative performance differences when adding evidence, suggesting the evidence mechanism may have diminishing returns or even become counterproductive at larger scales.

The 1.7B anomaly makes sense though—the smaller model generates repetitive and hallucinated outputs without the evidence structure to guide it, so the evidence actually provides crucial scaffolding that helps organize the generation. For the larger models, this constraint might be less necessary since they're already more capable at coherent output generation.

Now I need to set up the analysis framework. I should be mindful of the constraint about not touching GPU resources, so I'll write the analysis script locally first and then transfer it, or use non-GPU execution methods. \"train_annotated.json\"))\n\n    def is_repetitive(raw_output, threshold=3):\n        pattern = r'\\{[^{}]+\\}'\n        matches = re.findall(pattern, raw_output)\n        if len(matches) < threshold:\n            return False\n        for i in range(len(matches) - threshold + 1):\n            if len(set(matches[i:i+threshold])) == 1:\n                return True\n        if len(matches) > 5 and len(set(matches)) / len(matches) < 0.5:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def filter_and_eval(preds, doc_ids):\n        ids = set(doc_ids)\n        sub_preds, sub_gold = [], []\n        for p in preds:\n            if p[\"doc_id\"] not in ids:\n                continue\n            for t in p[\"parsed_triples\"]:\n                if isinstance(t, dict):\n                    sub_preds.append({\n                        \"doc_id\": p[\"doc_id\"], \"head\": t.get(\"head\", \"\"),\n                        \"tail\": t.get(\"tail\", \"\"), \"relation\": t.get(\"relation\", \"\"),\n                    })\n            for t in p[\"gold_triples\"]:\n                if isinstance(t, dict):\n                    sub_gold.append({\n                        \"doc_id\": p[\"doc_id\"], \"head\": t.get(\"head\", \"\"),\n                        \"tail\": t.get(\"tail\", \"\"), \"relation\": t.get(\"relation\", \"\"),\n                    })\n        return evaluator.compute_f1(sub_preds, sub_gold)\n\n    for model_name, paths in SFT_PAIRS.items():\n        we_preds = load_predictions(paths[\"with_evi\"])\n        ne_preds = load_predictions(paths[\"no_evi\"])\n        if we_preds is None or ne_preds is None:\n            print(f\"\\n  {model_name}: Missing predictions, skipping\")\n            continue\n\n        # Tag repetitive\n        for p in we_preds:\n            p[\"repetitive\"] = is_repetitive(p[\"raw_output\"])\n        for p in ne_preds:\n            p[\"repetitive\"] = is_repetitive(p[\"raw_output\"])\n\n        we_bad = set(p[\"doc_id\"] for p in we_preds if p[\"truncated\"] or p[\"repetitive\"])\n        ne_bad = set(p[\"doc_id\"] for p in ne_preds if p[\"truncated\"] or p[\"repetitive\"])\n        all_ids = [p[\"doc_id\"] for p in ne_preds]\n        fair_ids = [d for d in all_ids if d not in (we_bad | ne_bad)]\n\n        we_trunc = sum(1 for p in we_preds if p[\"truncated\"])\n        ne_trunc = sum(1 for p in ne_preds if p[\"truncated\"])\n        we_rep = sum(1 for p in we_preds if p[\"repetitive\"])\n        ne_rep = sum(1 for p in ne_preds if p[\"repetitive\"])\n\n        print(f\"\\n{'─'*70}\")\n        print(f\"  {model_name}\")\n        print(f\"{'─'*70}\")\n        print(f\"  With-evi: trunc={we_trunc}, rep={we_rep}, bad={len(we_bad)}\")\n        print(f\"  No-evi:   trunc={ne_trunc}, rep={ne_rep}, bad={len(ne_bad)}\")\n        print(f\"  Fair subset: {len(fair_ids)}/{len(all_ids)} docs\")\n\n        # Eval on full and fair\n        full_we = filter_and_eval(we_preds, all_ids)\n        full_ne = filter_and_eval(ne_preds, all_ids)\n        fair_we = filter_and_eval(we_preds, fair_ids)\n        fair_ne = filter_and_eval(ne_preds, fair_ids)\n\n        full_tax = (full_ne[\"ign_f1\"] - full_we[\"ign_f1\"]) * 100\n        fair_tax = (fair_ne[\"ign_f1\"] - fair_we[\"ign_f1\"]) * 100\n\n        print(f\"\\n  Full set:  WE Ign-F1={full_we['ign_f1']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"NE Ign-F1={full_ne['ign_f1']:.4f}  Tax={full_tax:+.2f}pp\")\n        print(f\"  Fair set:  WE Ign-F1={fair_we['ign_f1']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"NE Ign-F1={fair_ne['ign_f1']:.4f}  Tax={fair_tax:+.2f}pp\")\n        print(f\"  Tax change: {full_tax:.2f} -> {fair_tax:.2f}pp \"\n              f\"({'reduced' if abs(fair_tax) < abs(full_tax) else 'increased'})\")\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Main\n# ============================================================\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--module\", default=\"all\",\n                        choices=[\"A\", \"B\", \"C\", \"D\", \"E\", \"all\"],\n                        help=\"Which analysis module to run\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    modules = {\n        \"A\": module_a_output_length,\n        \"B\": module_b_truncation_impact,\n        \"C\": module_c_per_relation,\n        \"D\": module_d_false_positives,\n        \"E\": module_e_fair_subset,\n    }\n\n    if args.module == \"all\":\n        for name, func in modules.items():\n            try:\n                func()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"\\n  [ERROR] Module {name}: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    else:\n        modules[args.module]()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPTEOF\necho \"Script written successfully\"",
  "account": "root"
}

脚本语法通过。下面是汇报。

---

## 1. 已收集数据汇总表

### SFT with-evidence vs no-evidence

| Model | Mode | Rel F1 | Ign F1 | Evi F1 | EDCR | Preds | Trunc (%) | Tax (Ign-F1) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Qwen3-1.7B** | with-evi | 49.45 | 47.86 | 83.16 | 0.670 | 17184 | 314 (31.9%) | |
| | no-evi | 41.03 | 39.82 | — | — | 26157 | 501 (50.9%) | **-8.04pp** (evidence helps!) |
| **Qwen3-4B** | with-evi | 40.49 | 39.74 | 79.86 | 0.698 | 16880 | 288 (29.2%) | |
| | no-evi | 48.76 | 47.21 | — | — | 21181 | 307 (31.2%) | **+7.47pp** |
| **Qwen3-8B** | with-evi | 52.62 | 50.51 | 81.88 | 0.651 | 17401 | 313 (31.8%) | |
| | no-evi | 52.83 | 50.79 | — | — | 19948 | 225 (22.8%) | **+0.28pp** |
| **LLaMA-8B** | with-evi | 55.14 | 53.84 | 81.51 | 0.628 | 15325 | 197 (20.0%) | |
| | no-evi | 56.24 | 54.52 | — | — | 18466 | 182 (18.5%) | **+0.68pp** |

### RSFT (with-evidence only)

| Model | Ign F1 | Trunc | Preds |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1.7B RSFT-k1 (D102) | 51.22 | 249 | 16154 |
| 4B RSFT-CED s44 | 45.11 | 190 | 15264 |
| 8B RSFT | 54.33 | 267 | 16831 |
| LLaMA-8B RSFT | 55.63 | 187 | 14959 |

### 关键发现

**Evidence tax 模式非单调**：
- 1.7B: evidence 反而 **帮助** RE（-8.04pp），因为 no-evidence 1.7B 严重过度生成（26157 preds，50.9% 截断）
- 4B: evidence tax 最大（+7.47pp）
- 8B: evidence tax 几乎消失（+0.28~0.68pp）

这意味着 evidence tax 的核心机制是 **output length vs model capacity 的竞争**：
- 小模型（1.7B）: capacity 不够，no-evidence 模式下反而产生更多重复/幻觉，evidence 结构起到约束作用
- 中等模型（4B）: evidence 产生真正的 length tax，截断导致关系丢失
- 大模型（8B）: capacity 足够，evidence 几乎不影响 RE 性能

## 2. 缺失数据

| 数据 | 状态 |
|---|---|
| 4B with-evidence predictions.json | **缺失**（sft_baseline_d076_reeval 只有 metrics.json，无法做 per-doc 分析） |
| D102 8B RSFT eval (d102_8b_k1_s42) | **空目录**（eval 未开始/进行中） |
| D102 LLaMA RSFT eval (d102_llama_k1_s42) | **空目录** |
| D102 8B seed43 (d102_8b_k1_s43) | **空目录** |
| No-evidence RSFT 变体 | **不存在**（所有 RSFT 均 with-evidence） |

## 3. 分析脚本

路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/evidence_tax_analysis.py`

5 个模块：
- **Module A**: Output length distribution（chars, triples, 按 doc length 分层）
- **Module B**: Truncation impact（trunc vs clean docs 的 F1 差异，用 evaluator 重新计算）
- **Module C**: Per-relation analysis（按 relation type 计算 recall，找出 tax 最大的关系类型）
- **Module D**: False positive analysis（FP rate, FP 在 trunc vs clean docs 的分布）
- **Module E**: Fair subset analysis（排除 truncated+repetitive docs 后重新计算 tax）

运行：`python scripts/evidence_tax_analysis.py --module all`（或 A/B/C/D/E）

## 4. D102 eval 完成后还需什么

1. **重跑 4B with-evidence eval 并保存 predictions.json**，否则 4B 的 per-doc 分析无法进行
2. D102 8B/LLaMA eval 完成后，可以比较 RSFT 是否改变 evidence tax 模式
3. 可能需要 **4B no-evidence RSFT** 做完整的 RSFT-level tax 对比（目前只有 SFT-level 的对比）
